\displaystyle(3+1)\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{3}\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\hat{Q}_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},w_{i}(K))-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(iii)}{=}

\displaystyle 4\left\{\frac{1}{3}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\hat{Q}_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},w_{i}(K))-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\right]}_{(a)}+\underbrace{\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}}_{(b)}\right\}.

(32)

Here
(i)
uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,
(ii)
uses the fact that
\max_{x}\left\{f(x)+g(x)\right\}\leq\max_{x}f(x)+\max_{x}g(x)
for any two functions
f(x),g(x)
.
For
(iii)
, note that
Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})
is the shorthand notation of
Q_{i}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s,a_{i})
, and the term
(b)
is irrelevant to the trajectory sampled.

(a)
is the policy evaluation error in estimating the value function of policy
\hat{\xi}
, which can be derived using the result in Appendix
C
.
(b)
is the difference in the value function between policy
\hat{\xi}
and
\xi^{\theta}
, which can be bounded by a function of
\epsilon
.

Bound
(a)
.

With all assumptions in Appendix
C
satisfied (Appendix
D.2
), the following result is a direct application of Theorem
C.1
in localized TD(
\lambda
) with linear function approximation:

Corollary D.2
.

Choose step size
\alpha
such that
\alpha t_{\alpha}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1-\lambda}{4(1+\gamma)},\frac{(1-\gamma)(1-\lambda)^{2}\underline{\lambda}}{114(1+\gamma)^{2}}\right\}
.
Then for
i_{0}\geq t_{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\hat{Q}_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},w_{i}(K))-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\left[c_{1}^{*}(1-(1-\gamma)\underline{\lambda}\alpha)^{K-t_{\alpha}}+c_{2}^{*}\frac{\alpha t_{\alpha}}{(1-\gamma)\underline{\lambda}}+\epsilon_{i,red}^{2}+\left(\frac{\gamma^{\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}\right].

Here

\displaystyle c_{1}^{*}=\;

\displaystyle(\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|+1)^{2},\;c_{2}^{*}=114\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{1-\lambda}\right)^{2}(\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\left\lVert w_{i}^{*}\right\rVert+1)^{2},

\displaystyle\epsilon_{i,red}=\;

\displaystyle\sup_{s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i}}\left\lvert\hat{Q}_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},w_{i}^{*})-\tilde{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\right\rvert.

For localized TD(
\lambda
) with linear function approximation, we can associate
\epsilon_{i,red}
with function approximation error
\epsilon_{app}
in a similar way as
Tsitsiklis and Van Roy, 1997
.

Lemma D.5
.

The reduction error of agent
i
’ can be bounded by the function approximation error

\displaystyle\epsilon_{i,red}\leq\frac{1}{\pi_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1-\lambda\gamma}{1-\gamma}\epsilon_{app}.

Proof of Lemma
D.5
.

Denote by
\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}\in\Delta(\mathcal{S}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}\times\mathcal{A}_{i})
the marginal stationary distribution of
(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})
.
Define

\displaystyle\tilde{D}=\mathop{\textup{diag}}\limits_{(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\in\mathcal{S}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}}\{\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\}.

Then

\displaystyle\epsilon_{i,red}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\min_{s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i}}\{\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\}}\left\lVert\hat{Q}_{i}(\cdot,\cdot,w^{*})-\tilde{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\rVert_{\tilde{D}}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}\frac{1}{\min_{s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i}}\{\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\}}\cdot\frac{1-\lambda\gamma}{1-\gamma}\inf_{w}\left\lVert\hat{Q}_{i}(\cdot,\cdot,w)-\tilde{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\rVert_{\tilde{D}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\min_{s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i}}\{\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\}}\cdot\frac{1-\lambda\gamma}{1-\gamma}\inf_{w}\sup_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\hat{Q}_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},w)-\tilde{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\min_{s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i}}\{\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\}}\cdot\frac{1-\lambda\gamma}{1-\gamma}\epsilon_{app}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}\frac{1}{\pi_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1-\lambda\gamma}{1-\gamma}\epsilon_{app}.

Here in
(i)
, we applied
(
Tsitsiklis and Van Roy, 1997
, Lemma 6)
to the sub-chain
\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\hat{\xi}}
, and in
(ii)
, we used the fact that
\min_{s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i}}\{\overline{\pi}^{\hat{\xi}}_{\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\geq\pi_{\min}
.
∎

Combining Lemma
D.5
and Corollary
D.2
, we immediately have the bound of
(a)
.

Corollary D.3
.

Choose step size
\alpha
such that
\alpha t_{\alpha}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1-\lambda}{4(1+\gamma)},\frac{(1-\gamma)(1-\lambda)^{2}\underline{\lambda}}{114(1+\gamma)^{2}}\right\}
.
Then for
K\geq t_{\alpha}
and any
i\in\mathcal{N}
, we have

\displaystyle(a)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\hat{Q}_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},w(K))-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\left[c_{1}^{*}(1-(1-\gamma)\underline{\lambda}\alpha)^{K-t_{\alpha}}+c_{2}^{*}\frac{\alpha t_{\alpha}}{(1-\gamma)\underline{\lambda}}+\left(\frac{(1-\lambda\gamma)\epsilon_{app}}{\pi_{\min}(1-\gamma)}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{\gamma^{\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}}{1-\gamma}\right)\right]^{2}.

Here
c_{1}^{*}=(\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\|w_{i}^{*}\|+1)^{2}
,
c_{2}^{*}=114\left(\frac{1+\gamma}{1-\lambda}\right)^{2}(\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\left\lVert w_{i}^{*}\right\rVert+1)^{2}
.

Bound
(b)
.

We first discuss the
l_{1}
-distance of policy
\xi^{\theta}
and
\hat{\xi}
with the two lemmas belows.

Lemma D.6
.

For any agent
i
and policy
\xi_{i}
, let
\hat{\xi}_{i}(a_{i},s_{i})=(1-\epsilon)\xi_{i}(a_{i}|s_{i})+\epsilon\frac{1}{\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{i}\right\rvert}
for all
s_{i},a_{i}
. Then

\displaystyle\left\lVert\xi_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})-\hat{\xi}_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})\right\rVert_{1}\leq 2\epsilon,\;\forall s_{i}.

Proof of Lemma
D.6
.

We have by definition of the
l_{1}
-norm that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\xi_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})-\hat{\xi}_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})\right\rVert_{1}=

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{i}}\left\lvert\xi_{i}(a_{i}|s_{i})-\hat{\xi}_{i}(a_{i}|s_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\epsilon\sum_{a_{i}}\left\lvert\frac{1}{\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{i}\right\rvert}-\xi_{i}(a_{i}|s_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon\sum_{a_{i}}\left(\frac{1}{\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{i}\right\rvert}+\xi_{i}(a_{i}|s_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\epsilon.

∎

Consider the difference of policy of a set of agents, we have the following result.

Lemma D.7
.

For any set of agents
\mathcal{I}\subseteq\mathcal{N}
and any policy
\xi_{\mathcal{I}}
. Define
\hat{\xi}_{i}
in the same way as in Lemma
D.6
for any
i\in\mathcal{I}
and let
\hat{\xi}_{\mathcal{I}}
be the product policy. Then

\displaystyle\left\lVert\xi_{\mathcal{I}}(\cdot|s_{\mathcal{I}})-\hat{\xi}_{\mathcal{I}}(\cdot|s_{\mathcal{I}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq 2\left\lvert\mathcal{I}\right\rvert\epsilon,\;\forall s_{\mathcal{I}}.

Proof of Lemma
D.7
.

By Lemma
F.13
and Lemma
D.6
, we immediately have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\xi_{\mathcal{I}}(\cdot|s_{\mathcal{I}})-\hat{\xi}_{\mathcal{I}}(\cdot|s_{\mathcal{I}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\left\lVert\xi_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})-\hat{\xi}_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\lvert\mathcal{I}\right\rvert\epsilon.

∎

Now we are ready to bound
(b)
.

Lemma D.8
.

For any policy
\xi^{\theta}
, we have

\displaystyle(b)=\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\leq\left(\frac{6n\epsilon}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)^{2}.

Here
\hat{\xi}
is the
\epsilon
-exploration policy of policy
\xi^{\theta}
. Please refer to Line
2
of Algorithm
2
for the explicit definition of
\epsilon
-exploration policy.

Proof of Lemma
D.8
.

Notice that for any policy
\xi
, we have

\displaystyle Q_{i}^{\xi}(s,a_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{-k}}\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})Q_{i}^{\xi}(s,a_{i},a_{-k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{-k}}\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\left(r_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}},a_{i},a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}U_{i_{0}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}})+\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})\right).

Then we have for any two policies
\xi
and
\xi^{\prime}
that

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{a_{-k}}\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\left(r_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}},a_{i},a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}U_{i_{0}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}})+\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})\right)\right.

\displaystyle-\left.\sum_{a_{-k}}\xi_{-k}^{\prime}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\left(r_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}},a_{i},a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}U_{i_{0}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}})+\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{a_{-k}}(\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k}))r_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}},a_{i},a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}U_{i_{0}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}})\right\rvert

\displaystyle+\left\lvert\sum_{a_{-k},s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})\left[\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{a_{-k}}(\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k}))r_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}},a_{i},a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}U_{i_{0}}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}})\right\rvert

\displaystyle+\left\lvert\sum_{a_{-k},s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})\left[\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\right]V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})\right\rvert

\displaystyle+\left\lvert\sum_{a_{-k},s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})\xi_{-k}^{\prime}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\left[V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})-V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{-k}}\left\lvert\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\right\rvert

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{a_{-k}}\left\lvert\xi_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(a_{-k}|s_{-k})\right\rvert\sum_{s^{\prime}}\mathcal{P}(s^{\prime}|s,a_{i},a_{-k})

\displaystyle+\sup_{s^{\prime}}\left[V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})-V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\right].

To bound the last term, we apply a variant of performance difference lemma (Lemma
F.11
), and we have for any state
s
that

\displaystyle\left\lvert V_{i}^{\xi}(s)-V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s)\right\rvert=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left\lvert\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})(\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})-\xi(a|s^{\prime}))Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\left\lvert\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})(\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})-\xi(a|s^{\prime}))\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\left\lvert\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})-\xi(a|s^{\prime})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sup_{s^{\prime}}\left\lVert\xi^{\prime}(\cdot|s^{\prime})-\xi(\cdot|s^{\prime})\right\rVert_{1}.

(33)

By Eq. (
33
), we can further bound
\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert
as

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert\leq\;

\displaystyle\left\lVert\xi_{-k}(\cdot|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(\cdot|s_{-k})\right\rVert_{1}+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left\lVert\xi_{-k}(\cdot|s_{-k})-\xi^{\prime}_{-k}(\cdot|s_{-k})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sup_{s^{\prime\prime}}\left\lVert\xi^{\prime}(\cdot|s^{\prime\prime})-\xi(\cdot|s^{\prime\prime})\right\rVert_{1}.

In particular, by choosing
\xi\leftarrow\hat{\xi}
,
\xi^{\prime}\leftarrow\xi^{\theta}
and applying Lemma
D.7
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert\leq

\displaystyle 2(n-1)\epsilon+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\cdot 2(n-1)\epsilon+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\cdot 2n\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6n\epsilon}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Taking maximization on both sides w.r.t.
s
and
a_{i}
, we get

\displaystyle\max_{s,a_{i}}\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\hat{\xi}}(s,a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert^{2}\leq\left(\frac{6n\epsilon}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)^{2}.

∎

Finally, apply Corollary
D.3
and Lemma
D.8
to Eq. (
32
), and we complete the proof.

Appendix E
Proof of Theorem
5.2

For any
i\in\mathcal{N}
and
m\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}(\theta(m+1))-\Phi_{i}(\theta(m)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\left[\Phi_{i}(\theta(m+1))-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m+1),\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m))\right]

\displaystyle+\left[\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m+1),\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m))-\Phi_{i}(\theta(m))\right].

(34)

Similar to the proof of Theorem
5.1
, using Assumption
5.1
and the first term on the RHS of Eq. (
34
) can be lower-bounded as

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}(\theta(m+1))-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m+1),\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m))\geq-\frac{\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)\beta}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Now consider the second term. Denote
e_{j}(m)=\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))-\Delta_{j}^{T}(m)
. Using the smoothness property (Lemma
B.2
) of the local potential functions and we have

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m+1),\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m))-\Phi_{i}(\theta(m))

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\langle\nabla_{\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}\Phi_{i}(\theta(m)),\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m+1)-\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m)\rangle-\frac{L(\kappa)}{2}\left\lVert\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m+1)-\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(m)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left[\beta\langle\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m)),\Delta_{j}^{T}(m)\rangle-\frac{L(\kappa)\beta^{2}}{2}\left\lVert\Delta_{j}^{T}(m)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left[\beta\langle\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m)),\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))+e_{j}(m)\rangle-\frac{L(\kappa)\beta^{2}}{2}\left\lVert\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m)+e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left[(\beta-L(\kappa)\beta^{2})\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}+\beta\langle\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m)),e_{j}(m)\rangle-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}\right],

where the last line follows from
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
for any
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
. Using the previous two inequalities in Eq. (
34
) and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}(\theta(m+1))\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]-\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}(\theta(m)]

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)\beta}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+(\beta-L(\kappa)\beta^{2})\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\beta\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\langle\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m)),\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\rangle-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)\beta}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+(\beta-L(\kappa)\beta^{2})\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta}{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}(\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}+\|\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|^{2})-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)\beta}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\right)\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta}{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\|\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|^{2}-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}],

where
\mathcal{F}_{m}
represents the history up to the beginning of the
m
-th outer-loop iteration. Taking the total expectation on both sides of the previous inequality and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}(\theta(m+1))]-\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}(\theta(m)]

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)\beta}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\right)\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}]

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta}{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|^{2}]-L(\kappa)\beta^{2}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}],

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}(\theta(M))]-\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}(\theta(0)]

\displaystyle\geq\;

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)\beta}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{(\beta/2-L(\kappa)\beta^{2})}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}]

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta}{2M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|^{2}]-\frac{L(\kappa)\beta^{2}}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}].

(35)

Since
\Phi_{\min}\leq\Phi_{i}(\theta)\leq\Phi_{\max}
for all
\theta
and
\beta\leq\frac{1}{4L(\kappa)}
, after rearranging terms and we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4(\Phi_{\max}-\Phi_{\min})}{\beta M}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{2}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|^{2}]}_{\mathcal{T}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4L(\kappa)\beta}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert e_{j}(m)\right\rVert^{2}]}_{\mathcal{T}_{2}}.

Next, we bound the terms
\mathcal{T}_{1}
and
\mathcal{T}_{2}
from above. To begin with, we decompose
e_{i}(m)
in the following way:

\displaystyle e_{i}(m)=\;

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta(m))-\Delta_{i}^{T}(m)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}(m)}(a_{i}(k)|s_{i}(k))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta(m)}(s(k),a_{i}(k))\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{k=0}^{H-1}\gamma^{k}\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}(m)}(a_{i}^{t}(k)|s_{i}^{t}(k))\phi_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{c}}}^{t}(k),a_{i}^{t}(k))^{\top}w_{i}^{m}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{k=H}^{\infty}\gamma^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}(m)}(a_{i}(k)|s_{i}(k))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta(m)}(s(k),a_{i}(k))\right]

(
\mathcal{X}_{1}
)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{H-1}\gamma^{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}(m)}(a_{i}(k)|s_{i}(k))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta(m)}(s(k),a_{i}(k))\right]

(
\mathcal{X}_{2}
)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{k=0}^{H-1}\gamma^{k}\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}(m)}(a_{i}^{t}(k)|s_{i}^{t}(k))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta(m)}(s^{t}(k),a_{i}^{t}(k))

(
\mathcal{X}_{3}
)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{k=0}^{H-1}\gamma^{k}\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}(m)}(a_{i}^{t}(k)|s_{i}^{t}(k))

\displaystyle\times(\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta(m)}(s^{t}(k),a_{i}^{t}(k))-\phi_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{c}}}^{t}(k),a_{i}^{t}(k))^{\top}w_{i}^{m}).

(
\mathcal{X}_{4}
)

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[e_{i}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]=\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{1}+\mathcal{X}_{2}+\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{X}_{3}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]+\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{X}_{4}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{1}+\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{X}_{4}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}].

Therefore, using Lemma
F.7
, the definition of
\epsilon_{\text{critic}}
, and the fact that the averaged
Q
-function is bounded (in
\ell_{\infty}
-norm) by
\frac{1}{1-\gamma}
, and we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[e_{i}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|\leq\;

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{X}_{1}\|+\|\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{X}_{4}\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}\gamma^{H}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}\epsilon_{\text{critic}}}{1-\gamma},

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{1}=\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbb{E}[e_{j}(m)\mid\mathcal{F}_{m}]\|^{2}]\leq\frac{4\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}+\frac{4\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

As for the term
\mathcal{T}_{2}
, similarly we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2}=\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|e_{j}(m)\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{X}_{1}+\mathcal{X}_{2}+\mathcal{X}_{3}+\mathcal{X}_{4}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle 3\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{X}_{1}\|^{2}+\|\mathcal{X}_{2}+\mathcal{X}_{3}\|^{2}+\|\mathcal{X}_{4}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{6\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}+\frac{24}{(1-\gamma)^{2}T}+\frac{6\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Substituting the upper bounds we obtained for the terms
\mathcal{T}_{1}
and
\mathcal{T}_{2}
in Eq. (
35
) and we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4(\Phi_{\max}-\Phi_{\min})}{\beta M}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{8n(\kappa)\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}+\frac{8n(\kappa)\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+24L(\kappa)n(\kappa)\beta\left(\frac{\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}T}\right).

Recall that Lemma
B.4
implies

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lVert\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta(m))\right\rVert^{2}\geq\left\lVert\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta(m))\right\rVert^{2}\geq\frac{c^{2}}{\max_{j\in\mathcal{N}}|\mathcal{A}_{j}|D^{2}}\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\theta(m))^{2}.

Therefore, by choosing
\kappa=\kappa_{G}
and
\beta=\frac{1}{8L(\kappa)}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\mathbb{E}[\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\theta(m))^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{j\in\mathcal{N}}|\mathcal{A}_{j}|D^{2}}{c^{2}}\bigg[\frac{4(\Phi_{\max}-\Phi_{\min})}{\beta M}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}\nu(\kappa)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{8n(\kappa)\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}+\frac{8n(\kappa)\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+24L(\kappa)n(\kappa)\beta\left(\frac{\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{4}{(1-\gamma)^{2}T}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{12n(\kappa_{G})\max_{j\in\mathcal{N}}|\mathcal{A}_{j}|D^{2}}{c^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\bigg[\frac{16(\Phi_{\max}-\Phi_{\min})}{M(1-\gamma)}+\frac{\nu(\kappa_{G})}{n(\kappa_{G})}+\frac{\gamma^{2H}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}_{\text{critic}}+\frac{1}{T}\bigg].

Finally, using Jensen’s inequality and we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{Avg-Nash-Regret}}_{i}(M)]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\mathbb{E}[\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\theta(m))]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{4n(\kappa_{G})^{1/2}\max_{j\in\mathcal{N}}\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}_{j}|}}{c(1-\gamma)}\bigg[\frac{4(\Phi_{\max}-\Phi_{\min})^{1/2}}{M^{1/2}(1-\gamma)^{1/2}}+\frac{\nu(\kappa_{G})^{1/2}}{n(\kappa_{G})^{1/2}}+\frac{\gamma^{H}}{1-\gamma}+\epsilon_{\text{critic}}+\frac{1}{T^{1/2}}\bigg].

Since the RHS of the bound is not a function of
i
, we have the desired result.

Appendix F
Supporting Results

F.1  NMPG is a strict generalization of MPG

We present a simple example to show that NMPG is a strict generalization of the standard MPG.

Example:
Consider a networked multi-agent Markov game, denoted by
MG
, with 4 agents
\mathcal{N}=\{1,2,3,4\}
. Each agent has local state space
\mathcal{S}_{i}=\{s_{b},s_{g}\}
and local action space
\mathcal{A}_{i}=\{a_{b},a_{g}\}
for all agent
i\in\mathcal{N}
. The underlying undirected graph connects edges between every “neighboring” agents, i.e., the set of edges is
\mathcal{E}=\left\{(1,2),(2,3),(3,4)\right\}
.

•

Initial state: All agents starts at state
s_{b}
. That is,
s_{i}(0)=s_{b}
for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
.

•

Transition:
MG
has deterministic transitions. For agent 1, the next state only depends on its own action: For all
t\geq 0
,
s_{1}(t+1)=s_{g}
if
a_{1}(t)=a_{g}
and
s_{1}(t+1)=s_{b}
if
a_{1}(t)=a_{b}
. For agent
i\in\{2,3,4\}
, the next state only depends on state of "previous" agent, i.e.,
s_{i}(t+1)=s_{i-1}(t)
, for all
t\geq 0
.

•

Reward: Each agent’s reward is only dependent on its own state and action. The local reward of agent 1,2,3 is always 0. That is,
r_{i}(s_{i},a_{i})=0
for all
i\in\left\{1,2,3\right\}
,
s_{i}\in\mathcal{S}_{i}
,
a_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}
. For agent 4, it receives reward 1 if it is at state
s_{g}
and takes action
a_{g}
while it receives reward 0 in all other cases. In other words,
r_{4}(s_{g},a_{g})=1
,
r_{4}(s_{g},a_{b})=0
,
r_{4}(s_{b},a_{g})=0
,
r_{4}(s_{b},a_{b})=0
.

Obviously, the expected return of agent 1,2 and 3 is always 0. As for agent 4, its state is solely dependent on the state of agent 1 (three timesteps before), which is completely determined by agent 1’s policy. As a result, agent 4’s expected return, or objective function, depends on local policies of
both
agent 1 and 4. This observation is the key to showing that
MG
is not an MPG while being a 1-NMPG, which is summarized as the theorem below.

Theorem F.1
.

MG
is a 1-NMPG but not an MPG.

Proof of Theorem
F.1
.

We first compute the objective function
J_{i}(\xi)
for all global policy
\xi
and agent
i\in\mathcal{N}
. Noticing that agents 1,2,3 always receive reward 0, we have
J_{i}(\xi)=0
for all
i\in\left\{1,2,3\right\}
. As for agent 4, we have

\displaystyle J_{4}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\left[r_{4}(s_{4}(t),a_{4}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\Pr[r_{4}(s_{4}(t),a_{4}(t)=1]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\Pr[s_{4}(t)=s_{g},a_{4}(t)=a_{g}]

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{t=4}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\Pr[a_{1}(t-4)=a_{g},a_{4}(t)=a_{g}]

(36)

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle f(\xi_{1},\xi_{4}).

Here
(i)
uses the fact that
s_{4}(t)=s_{b}
for
t=0,1,2,3
, and
s_{4}(t)=s_{1}(t-3)=s_{g}
if and only if
a_{1}(t-4)=a_{g}
for all
t\geq 4
.

Next we show that
MG
is a 1-NMPG. In fact, we can choose local potential functions
\Phi_{1}(\xi)=\Phi_{2}(\xi)=0
,
\Phi_{3}(\xi)=\Phi_{4}(\xi)=f(\xi_{1},\xi_{4})
for all global policy
\xi
, and Eq. (
2
) can be satisfied.

Finally, we prove that
MG
is not an MPG. Assume that there is a potential
\Phi(\xi)
such that

\displaystyle J_{i}(\xi_{i}^{\prime},\xi_{-i})-J_{i}({\xi_{i},\xi_{-i}})=\Phi(\xi_{i}^{\prime},\xi_{-i})-\Phi(\xi_{i},\xi_{-i})

(37)

for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
,
\xi_{i},\xi_{i}^{\prime}\in\Xi_{i}
,
\xi_{-i}\in\Xi_{-i}
. If we choose
i\in\{1,2,3\}
in Eq. (
37
), we can see that
\Phi(\xi_{i}^{\prime},\xi_{-i})-\Phi(\xi_{i},\xi_{-i})=0
for all
\xi_{i},\xi_{i}^{\prime}\in\Xi_{i}
,
\xi_{-i}\in\Xi_{-i}
. Thus
\Phi
is independent of
\xi_{i}
for all
i\in\left\{1,2,3\right\}
. As a result, the potential function can be represented as
\Phi(\xi_{4})
. Then we let
i=4
in Eq. (
37
), and we have for all
\xi_{1}^{\prime},\xi_{1}\in\Xi_{1}
,
\xi_{4}^{\prime}\in\Xi_{4}
that

\displaystyle\Phi(\xi_{4}^{\prime})-\Phi(\xi_{4})=J_{4}(\xi_{4}^{\prime},\xi_{-4})-J_{4}(\xi_{4},\xi_{-4})=f(\xi_{1},\xi_{4}^{\prime})-f(\xi_{1},\xi_{4}).

(38)

To derive a contradiction, we take some special values of
\xi_{1},\xi_{1}^{\prime},\xi_{4}
. For any
i\in\mathbb{N}
, let
\xi_{i}^{g}
be the policy that agent
i
always takes
a_{g}
and
\xi_{i}^{b}
be the policy that agent
i
always takes
a_{b}
. Then we can derive from Eq. (
36
) that
f(\xi_{1}^{g},\xi_{4}^{g})=\frac{\gamma^{4}}{1-\gamma}
,
f(\xi_{1}^{g},\xi_{4}^{b})=f(\xi_{1}^{b},\xi_{4}^{g})=f(\xi_{1}^{b},\xi_{4}^{b})=0
. In Eq. (
38
), let
\xi_{4}^{\prime}=\xi_{4}^{g}
,
\xi_{4}=\xi_{4}^{b}
,
\xi_{1}\in\{\xi_{1}^{b},\xi_{1}^{g}\}
, and we have

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\Phi(\xi_{4}^{g})-\Phi(\xi_{4}^{b})=f(\xi_{1}^{b},\xi_{4}^{g})-f(\xi_{1}^{b},\xi_{4}^{b})=0\\
\Phi(\xi_{4}^{g})-\Phi(\xi_{4}^{b})=f(\xi_{1}^{g},\xi_{4}^{g})-f(\xi_{1}^{g},\xi_{4}^{b})=\frac{\gamma^{4}}{1-\gamma},\end{cases}

which leads to contradiction. As a result,
MG
is not an MPG.
∎

\kappa_{G}
-NMPG in general cannot be reduced to a standard MPG. Please note that in a
\kappa_{G}
-NMPG, for any agent
i
, any agent
j\in N_{i}^{\kappa_{G}}
do share a potential function
\Phi_{i}
, but this potential is associated with the agent
i
. In the reviewer’s example, the potential functions
\Phi_{i_{1}},\Phi_{i_{2}},\cdots,\Phi_{i_{\kappa_{G}+1}}
are associated with agent
i_{1}
, while the potential functions
\Phi_{i_{L}},\Phi_{i_{L-1}},\cdots,\Phi_{i_{L-\kappa_{G}}}
are associated with agent
i_{L}
. Therefore, unless
L\leq 2\kappa_{G}-1
,
i_{1}
and
i_{L}
are not guaranteed to share a same potential function.

In addition, we can give a simple example that is a for your reference. The transition is deterministic. The initial states of the 4 agents are all
s_{b}
. Then
J_{1}(\xi)=J_{2}(\xi)=J_{3}(\xi)=0
.

J_{4}(\xi)=\sum_{t=4}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\Pr[a_{1}(t-4)=a_{g}]\Pr[a_{4}(t)=a_{g}]\triangleq f(\xi_{1},\xi_{4}).

The above example is a 1-NMPG, with
\Phi_{1}(\xi)=\Phi_{2}(\xi)=0
,
\Phi_{3}(\xi)=\Phi_{4}(\xi)=f(\xi_{1},\xi_{4})
, but it is not an MPG.

F.2  Policy Gradient Theorem Variant

We prove Eq. (
4
), which is a variant of the policy gradient theorem
(
Sutton et al., 1999
)
.

Lemma F.1

(Policy gradient theorem variant)
.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta)=\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\mathbb{E}_{\xi^{\theta}}\left[\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}(t)|s_{i}(t))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s(t),a_{i}(t))\right].

Proof of Lemma
F.1
.

By Lemma
F.8
, we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s,a_{i}}\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}{\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}[s(t)=s|s(0)\sim\mu(\cdot)]\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\sum_{s,a_{i}}\gamma^{t}{\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}[s(t)=s|s(0)\sim\mu(\cdot)]\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\mathbb{E}_{\xi^{\theta}}\left[\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\log\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}(t)|s_{i}(t))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s(t),a_{i}(t))\right].

Here
{\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}[s(t)=s|s(0)\sim\mu(\cdot)]
represents the probability that
s(t)=s
given that the policy is
\xi^{\theta}
and initial state
s(0)
is sampled from distribution
\mu
.
∎

F.3  Proof of Lemma
4.1

When
\kappa_{c}\leq\kappa_{r}-1
, the conclusion is obvious by the fact that both the truncated averaged
Q
-function and the averaged
Q
-function are in the range of
[0,\frac{1}{1-\gamma}]
. Below we only consider the case that
\kappa_{c}\geq\kappa_{r}
.

Consider any agent
i
, global policy parameter
\theta
, and any truncated averaged
Q
-function
\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta,\kappa_{c}}\in\mathcal{Q}_{i}^{\theta,\kappa_{c}}
. Then there exists
u_{i}\in\Delta(\mathcal{S}_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}})
, such that
\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta,\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})=\sum_{s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}}u_{i}(s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})
for any
(s_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}},a_{i})\in\mathcal{S}_{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}
. We have for any
(s,a_{i})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}_{i}
that

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta,\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime}}u_{i}(s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime})\left(\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime},a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\right)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime}}u_{i}(s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime})\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime},a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime}}\sum_{a_{-i}}u_{i}(s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime})\xi_{-i}^{\theta_{-i}}(a_{-i}|s_{-i})\left\lvert Q_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime},a_{i},a_{-i})-Q_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},a_{-i})\right\rvert.

(39)

We now try to give a perturbation bound for agent
i
’s
Q
-function w.r.t.
s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}
, the states of agents in
\kappa
-hop neighborhood.
Notice that for any
s\in\mathcal{S},a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\sum_{s(t),a(t)}{\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s(t)|s(0)=s,a(0)=a)\xi^{\theta}(a(t)|s(t))r_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\sum_{s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)}{\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)|s(0)=s,a(0)=a)r_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\sum_{s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)}{\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)|s(0)=s,a(0)=a)r_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))

for simplicity of notation.

For any fixed
i\in\mathcal{N}
,
s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}\in\mathcal{S}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}
,
s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}
,
a\in\mathcal{A}
and policy
\xi^{\theta}
, let

\displaystyle\pi_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))={\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)|s(0)=(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}),a(0)=a)

\displaystyle\tilde{\pi}_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))={\Pr}^{\xi^{\theta}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)|s(0)=(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime}),a(0)=a).

Due to the local dependence of network and localized policy structure,
\pi_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}
is only dependent on the initial states and actions of agents in
\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}+t}
, which is equal to
\tilde{\pi}_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}
when
t\leq\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}
. Therefore we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert Q_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime},a_{i},a_{-i})-Q_{i}^{\theta}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i},a_{-i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\sum_{s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)}\left(\xi_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))-\tilde{\pi}_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times r_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\sum_{s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t)}\left\lvert\xi_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))-\tilde{\pi}_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))\right\rvert

\displaystyle\times r_{i}(s_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t),a_{\mathcal{N}_{i}^{\kappa_{r}}}(t))

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\left\lVert\pi_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}-\tilde{\pi}_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\left\lVert\pi_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}-\tilde{\pi}_{t}^{\kappa_{r}}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{t=\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\gamma^{\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}.

Here
(i)
is by
r_{i}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}},a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}})\leq 1
for any
i\in\mathcal{N}
and any
s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}}
,
a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{r}}}}
. Plug into Eq. (
39
), and we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta,\kappa_{c}}(s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}},a_{i})-\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime}}\sum_{a_{-i}}u_{i}(s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{c}}}}^{\prime})\xi_{-i}^{\theta_{-i}}(a_{-i}|s_{-i})\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\gamma^{\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{1-\gamma}\gamma^{\kappa_{c}-\kappa_{r}+1}.

Take
\max
over
s,a_{i}
,
\sup
over
\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta,\kappa_{c}}
, and we complete the proof.

F.4  Averaged Nash Regret

The relationship between the averaged Nash regret defined in this work and the Nash regret in
Ding et al., 2022
is shown in the following lemma. Recall that we denote
n=|\mathcal{N}|
.

Lemma F.2
.

Given any positive integer
M
, the following inequality holds for any sequence of policies
\{\xi(0),\xi(1),\cdots,\xi(M-1)\}
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\text{Nash-Regret}(M)\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{Avg-Nash-Regret}}(M)\leq\text{Nash-Regret}(M).

Proof of Lemma
F.2
.

By definition of the averaged Nash Regret (cf. Definition
3.4
) and the Nash Regret (cf. Eq. (
3
)), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\text{Nash-Regret}(M)=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\xi(m))

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\xi(m))

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\xi(m))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{Avg-Nash-Regret}}(M)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\max_{i\in\mathcal{N}}\text{NE-Gap}_{i}(\xi(m))

(Jensen’s inequality)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\text{Nash-Regret}(M).

∎

F.5  Decay of Local Potential Functions

We derive
\nu(\kappa)=O(\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{j}\right\rvert)
given a mild assumption,
which guarantees existence of stage potential.

Assumption F.1
.

For any agent
i\in\mathcal{N}
, there exists stage potential function
\varphi_{i}:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow[0,\overline{\varphi}]
, such that

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}(\theta)=\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\left[\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\gamma^{t}\varphi_{i}(s(t),a(t))\right].

(40)

This assumption is common in recent MPG literature
(
Zhang et al., 2022a
)
.

Lemma F.3
.

With Assumption
F.1
satisfied, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\prime})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\right\rvert\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\varphi}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{j}\right\rvert\max_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert.

(41)

Proof of Lemma
F.3
.

Similar to
Q
-function and averaged
Q
-function, we can define “
Q
-potential” function and averaged “
Q
-potential” function as

\displaystyle Q\Phi_{i}^{\theta}(s,a)=\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\left[\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\varphi_{i}(s(t),a(t))|s(0)=s,a(0)=a\right]

\displaystyle\overline{Q\Phi}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})=\sum_{a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}\xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}(a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})Q\Phi_{i}(s,a_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}).

In Lemma
F.12
, replace the objective function
J_{i}
with potential function
\Phi_{i}
, treat agents in
-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}
as one agent, and we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\prime})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\right\rvert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\left\lvert\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\prime}}(a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})-\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}(a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\right\rvert\overline{Q\Phi}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\varphi}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s,a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\left\lvert\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\prime}}(a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})-\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}(a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\right\rvert.

Here
(i)
uses the fact that
\overline{Q\Phi}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\leq\frac{\overline{\varphi}}{1-\gamma}
.
By Lagrange mean value theorem, for any
j
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\xi_{j}^{\theta_{j}^{\prime}}(a_{j}|s_{j})-\xi_{j}^{\theta_{j}}(a_{j}|s_{j})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,1],\hat{\theta}_{j}=t\theta_{j}^{\prime}+(1-t)\theta_{j}}\left\lVert\nabla_{\theta_{j}}\xi_{j}^{\hat{\theta}_{j}}(a_{j}|s_{j})\right\rVert\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert

By Lemma
F.13
, we derive that

\displaystyle\sum_{a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}\left\lvert\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\prime}}(a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})-\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}}(a_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}|s_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lVert\xi_{j}^{\theta^{\prime}_{j}}(\cdot|s_{j})-\xi_{j}^{\theta_{j}}(\cdot|s_{j})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\sum_{a_{i}}\left\lvert\xi_{j}^{\theta^{\prime}_{j}}(a_{j}|s_{j})-\xi_{j}^{\theta_{j}}(a_{j}|s_{j})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{j}\right\rvert\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{j}\right\rvert\max_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert.

Therefore, we arrive at the conclusion

\displaystyle\left\lvert\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}^{\prime})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}},\theta_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}})\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{\varphi}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\sqrt{2}\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{j}\right\rvert\max_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}\overline{\varphi}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lvert\mathcal{A}_{j}\right\rvert\max_{j\in-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}}}}\left\lVert\theta_{j}^{\prime}-\theta_{j}\right\rVert.

∎

F.6  Boundedness of Local Potential Functions

Lemma F.4
.

For any agent
i\in\mathcal{N}
, let
\xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}},\xi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}\in\Xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}
and
\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}\in\Xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}
be arbitrary. Then we have

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}(\xi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}},\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})-\Phi_{i}(\xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}},\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})\leq\frac{\left\lvert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}\right\rvert}{1-\gamma}.

Proof of Lemma
F.4
.

Suppose that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}=\{i_{1},i_{2},\cdots,i_{k}\}
, where
k=\left\lvert{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}\right\rvert
. For any
r\in\{1,2,\cdots,k+1\}
, denote
\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{r}=(\xi_{i_{1}},\cdots,\xi_{i_{r-1}},\xi^{\prime}_{i_{r}},\cdots,\xi^{\prime}_{i_{k}})
. Note that
\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{1}=\xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{\prime}
and
\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{k+1}=\xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}
. Then, we have by Definition
3.1
that

\displaystyle\Phi_{i}(\xi^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}},\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})-\Phi_{i}(\xi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}},\xi_{-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{k}\left[\Phi_{i}(\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{r},\xi_{\-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})-\Phi_{i}(\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{r+1},\xi_{\-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})\right]

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{k}\left[J_{i_{r}}(\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{r},\xi_{\-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})-J_{i_{r}}(\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{r+1},\xi_{\-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{k}J_{i_{r}}(\tilde{\xi}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}}^{r},\xi_{\-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}})

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{k}{1-\gamma}.

∎

Lemma F.5
.

For an arbitrary NMPG, there exist a set of local potential functions
\{\hat{\Phi}_{i}\}_{i\in\mathcal{N}}
and
\Phi_{\min},\Phi_{\max}>0
satisfying
0\leq\Phi_{\max}-\Phi_{\min}\leq\frac{2n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}
such that
\Phi_{\min}\leq\hat{\Phi}_{i}(\xi)\leq\Phi_{\max}
for all
i\in\mathcal{N}
and
\xi\in\Xi
.

Proof of Lemma
F.5
.

Let
\{\Phi_{i}\}_{i\in\mathcal{N}}
be a set of local potential functions, and let
\bar{\xi}\in\Xi
be an arbitrary policy. Define
\{\hat{\Phi}_{i}\}_{i\in\mathcal{N}}
as

\displaystyle\hat{\Phi}_{i}(\xi)=\Phi_{i}(\xi)-\Phi_{i}(\bar{\xi})+\frac{n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}+1

for all
i\in\mathcal{N}
and
\xi\in\Xi
. It can be easily verified that
\{\hat{\Phi}_{i}\}_{i\in\mathcal{N}}
is also a set of local potential functions. Now, for any
i\in\mathcal{N}
and
\xi\in\Xi
, we have by Lemma
F.4
that

\displaystyle\hat{\Phi}_{i}(\xi)=\;

\displaystyle\hat{\Phi}_{i}(\xi)-\hat{\Phi}_{i}(\bar{\xi})+\frac{n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}+1\leq\frac{2n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}+1,

\displaystyle\hat{\Phi}_{i}(\xi)=\;

\displaystyle-\left(\Phi_{i}(\bar{\xi})-\Phi_{i}(\xi)-\frac{n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}-1\right)\geq 1.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle 1\leq\Phi_{i}(\xi)\leq\frac{2n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}+1,\quad\forall\;i\in\mathcal{N},\xi\in\Xi.

The result follows by letting
\Phi_{\min}=1
and
\Phi_{\max}=\frac{2n(\kappa_{G})}{1-\gamma}+1
.
∎

F.7  Other Technical Lemmas

Lemma F.6
.

Under softmax parameterization with weights
\{\theta_{i}\}_{i\in\mathcal{N}}
,
the derivative of the policy
\xi^{\theta}
is given by

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})}{\partial\theta_{i,s_{i}^{\prime},a_{i}^{\prime}}}=\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\mathds{1}\{s_{i}^{\prime}=s_{i}\}\left(\mathds{1}\{a_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i}\}-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i})\right)

for all
i\in\mathcal{N}
,
s_{i},s_{i}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}_{i}
,
a_{i},a_{i}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}
, and
\theta_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}_{i}||\mathcal{A}_{i}|}
.

Proof of Lemma
F.6
.

When
s_{i}^{\prime}\neq s_{i}
, since
\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})
is not a function of
\theta_{i,s_{i}^{\prime},a_{i}^{\prime}}
, the result clearly holds. We next consider the case where
s_{i}^{\prime}=s_{i}
.

Given an arbitrary positive integer
d
, let
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}^{d}
be the softmax operator defined as

\displaystyle[f(x)](\ell)=\frac{\exp(x_{\ell})}{\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=1}^{d}\exp(x_{\ell^{\prime}})},\quad\forall\;\ell\in\{1,2,\cdots,d\}.

It was shown in
(
Gao and Pavel, 2017
, Proposition 2)
that
\nabla f(x)=\text{diag}(f(x))-f(x)f(x)^{\top}
.
Therefore, we have for any
a_{i}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}
that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})}{\partial\theta_{i,s_{i},a_{i}^{\prime}}}=\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\mathds{1}\{a_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i}\}-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i}).

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma F.7
.

For any softmax policy
\xi^{\theta}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\|\leq\sqrt{2}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\leq\sqrt{2},\quad\forall\;i,s_{i},a_{i},\theta_{i}.

Proof of Lemma
F.7
.

By Lemma
F.6
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\|=\;

\displaystyle\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\left[(1-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}))^{2}+\sum_{a_{i}^{\prime}\neq a_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i})^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\left[(1-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}))+\sum_{a_{i}^{\prime}\neq a_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i})\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}).

∎

Lemma F.8

(Multi-Agent Policy Gradient Theorem)
.

It holds for all
i\in\mathcal{N}
and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}|}
that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i}).

Proof of Lemma
F.8
.

Using the policy gradient theorem
(
Sutton et al., 1999
, Theorem 1)
and we have for any
i\in\mathcal{N}
that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta)=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi^{\theta}(a|s)Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}\sum_{a_{i}}\sum_{a_{-i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi^{\theta}(a_{i},a_{-i}|s)Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i},a_{-i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}\sum_{a_{i}}\sum_{a_{-i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}[\xi^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\xi^{\theta_{-i}}(a_{-i}|s_{-i})]Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i},a_{-i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s}\sum_{a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\sum_{a_{-i}}\xi^{\theta_{-i}}(a_{-i}|s_{-i})Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i},a_{-i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i}),

where the last line follows from the definition of the averaged
Q
-function.
∎

Lemma F.9
.

It holds for all
i\in\mathcal{N}
and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{S}||\mathcal{A}|}
that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta)\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Proof of Lemma
F.9
.

Using Lemma
F.7
and Lemma
F.8
, and we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\theta_{i}}J_{i}(\theta)\right\|=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left\lVert\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\left|\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})\right|\left\lVert\nabla_{\theta_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

∎

Lemma F.10
.

The following inequality holds for all
i
and
\theta
:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial J_{i}(\theta)}{\partial\theta_{i,s_{i}^{\prime},a_{i}^{\prime}}}=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s_{-i}}d^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i})\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i}^{\prime})\overline{A}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i},a_{i}^{\prime}).

Proof of Lemma
F.10
.

Using Lemma
F.6
and Lemma
F.8
, and we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial J_{i}(\theta)}{\partial\theta_{i,s_{i}^{\prime},a_{i}^{\prime}}}=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\frac{\partial\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})}{\partial\theta_{i,s_{i}^{\prime},a_{i}^{\prime}}}\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s)\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\mathds{1}\{s_{i}^{\prime}=s_{i}\}\left(\mathds{1}\{a_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i}\}-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i})\right)\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s_{-i},a_{i}}d^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i})\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}^{\prime})\left(\mathds{1}\{a_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i}\}-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i}^{\prime})\right)\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i},a_{i})

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s_{-i}}d^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i})\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i}^{\prime})\left(\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i},a_{i}^{\prime})-\sum_{a_{i}}\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}^{\prime})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i},a_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s_{-i}}d^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i})\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i}^{\prime})\left(\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i},a_{i}^{\prime})-V_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i})\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s_{-i}}d^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i})\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}^{\prime}|s_{i}^{\prime})\overline{A}_{i}^{\theta}(s_{i}^{\prime},s_{-i},a_{i}^{\prime}),

where the last line follows from the definition of the averaged advantage function.
∎

Lemma F.11
.

The following inequality holds for all
\xi,\xi^{\prime}\in\Xi
,
i\in\mathcal{N}
, and
s\in\mathcal{S}
:

\displaystyle V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s)-V_{i}^{\xi}(s)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})(\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})-\xi(a|s^{\prime}))Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a).

Proof of Lemma
F.11
.

Using the performance difference lemma in the single agent setting
(
Agarwal et al., 2021
, Lemma 2)
, and we have

\displaystyle V_{i}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s)-V_{i}^{\xi}(s)=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})A_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})(Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a)-V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime}))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a)-\sum_{s^{\prime}}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})V_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left(\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a)-\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s)\xi(a|s^{\prime})Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s^{\prime},a}d_{s}^{\xi^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})(\xi^{\prime}(a|s^{\prime})-\xi(a|s^{\prime}))Q_{i}^{\xi}(s^{\prime},a).

∎

Lemma F.12
.

It holds for any
i\in\mathcal{N}
,
\theta=(\theta_{i},\theta_{-i})
, and
\theta^{\prime}=(\theta_{i}^{\prime},\theta_{-i})
that

\displaystyle J_{i}(\theta^{\prime})-J_{i}(\theta)=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)(\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}^{\prime}}(a_{i}|s_{i})-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i}).

Proof of Lemma
F.12
.

Using the performance difference lemma in the single agent setting
(
Agarwal et al., 2021
, Lemma 2)
, and we have

\displaystyle J_{i}(\theta^{\prime})-J_{i}(\theta)=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\xi^{\theta^{\prime}}(a|s)A_{i}^{\theta}(s,a)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\xi^{\theta^{\prime}}(a|s)(Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a)-V_{i}^{\theta}(s))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left(\sum_{s,a_{i}}\sum_{a_{-i}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}^{\prime}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\xi_{-i}^{\theta_{-i}}(a_{-i}|s_{-i})Q_{i}^{\theta}(s,a)-\sum_{s}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)V_{i}^{\theta}(s)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left(\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}^{\prime}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})-\sum_{s}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)V_{i}^{\theta}(s)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left(\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}^{\prime}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})-\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i})\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\sum_{s,a_{i}}d^{\theta^{\prime}}(s)(\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}^{\prime}}(a_{i}|s_{i})-\xi_{i}^{\theta_{i}}(a_{i}|s_{i}))\overline{Q}_{i}^{\theta}(s,a_{i}).

∎

Lemma F.13
.

The following inequality holds for any
\mathcal{I}\subseteq\mathcal{N}
and any
\xi_{\mathcal{I}},\xi^{\prime}_{\mathcal{I}}\in\Xi_{\mathcal{I}}
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\xi_{\mathcal{I}}(\cdot|s_{\mathcal{I}})-\xi^{\prime}_{\mathcal{I}}(\cdot|s_{\mathcal{I}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}\left\lVert\xi_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})-\xi^{\prime}_{i}(\cdot|s_{i})\right\rVert_{1},\quad\forall\;s_{\mathcal{I}}\in\mathcal{S}_{\mathcal{I}}.

Proof of Lemma
F.13
.

The result follows by applying
(
Durrett, 2019
, Lemma 3.4.3)
.
∎

Lemma F.14

(Property of NMPG)
.

In an NMPG, consider any
i\in\mathcal{N}
and any policy parameter
\theta
. Then the following equality holds for any
j\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}N_{i}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\kappa}_{G}}}
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta)=\nabla_{\theta_{j}}\Phi_{i}(\theta).

Proof of Lemma
F.14
.

The proof essentially follows from
Leonardos et al., 2022
. Using the definition of NMPG (cf. Definition
3.1
), we have for any
\theta_{j},\theta_{j}^{\prime}
, and
\theta_{-j}
that

\displaystyle J_{j}(\theta_{j}^{\prime},\theta_{-j})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{j}^{\prime},\theta_{-j})=J_{j}({\theta_{j},\theta_{-j}})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{j},\theta_{-j}).

Thus
J_{j}({\theta_{j},\theta_{-j}})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{j},\theta_{-j})
is independent of
\theta_{j}
. Let
J_{j}({\theta_{j},\theta_{-j}})-\Phi_{i}(\theta_{j},\theta_{-j})=U_{j}(\theta_{-j})
. Taking gradient with respect to
\theta_{j}
on both sides, and we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta_{j}}J_{j}(\theta_{j},\theta_{-j})=\nabla_{\theta_{j}}\Phi_{i}(\theta_{j},\theta_{-j}).

∎
